function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
}

function compactList(arr, emptyLabel='すべて') {
  const vals = Array.from(arr || []).filter(v => String(v || '').trim());
  if (!vals.length) return emptyLabel;
  if (vals.length <= 6) return vals.join('、');
  return vals.slice(0, 6).join('、') + `、ほか${vals.length - 6}件`;
}
function currentDatasetLabel() {
  if (state.dataset === 'textbook') return '教科書版';
  if (state.dataset === 'exam') return '高校入試版';
  return '小学生版';
}
function quizModeTextForPrint() {
  if (els.starQuizRule && els.starQuizRule.value === 'star_both_nonstar_read') return '☆は読み・書き／それ以外は読みだけ';
  const opt = els.quizMode.options[els.quizMode.selectedIndex];
  return opt ? opt.textContent : '';
}
function buildRangeRowsForPrint() {
  const rows = [];
  rows.push(['モード', currentDatasetLabel()]);
  if (state.dataset === 'textbook') {
    rows.push(['教科書', compactList(state.filters.textbook, 'すべて')]);
    rows.push(['学年', compactList(state.filters.grade, 'すべて')]);
    rows.push(['大単元', compactList(state.filters.major, 'すべて')]);
    rows.push(['小単元', compactList(state.filters.section, 'すべて')]);
    rows.push(['品詞', compactList(state.filters.pos, 'すべて')]);
    const cond = [];
    if (state.toggles.starOnly) cond.push('☆のみ');
    if (state.toggles.phraseOnly) cond.push('熟語・表現のみ');
    if (state.toggles.currentOnly) cond.push('現行確認済みのみ');
    rows.push(['条件', cond.length ? cond.join('、') : '指定なし']);
  } else if (state.dataset === 'exam') {
    rows.push(['入試カテゴリ', compactList(state.filters.examCategory, 'すべて')]);
    rows.push(['分類', compactList(state.filters.examSub, 'すべて')]);
  } else {
    rows.push(['テスト区分', compactList(state.filters.elemType, 'すべて')]);
    rows.push(['カテゴリー', compactList(state.filters.elemCategory, 'すべて')]);
  }
  rows.push(['対象語数', `${state.currentList.length}語`]);
  rows.push(['出題形式', quizModeTextForPrint()]);
  rows.push(['問題数', state.currentTest.length ? `${state.currentTest.length}問` : '未作成']);
  return rows;
}
function normalizeGradeLabelForPrint(v) {
  const s = String(v || '').replace(/\.0$/, '').trim();
  if (!s) return '';
  return /^\d+$/.test(s) ? `中${s}` : s;
}
function uniqueFromCurrentList(key) {
  const out = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  (state.currentList || []).forEach(r => {
    const v = String(r[key] || '').replace(/\.0$/, '').trim();
    if (v && !seen.has(v)) { seen.add(v); out.push(v); }
  });
  return out;
}
function shortItemsForPrint(items, emptyLabel='全範囲') {
  const vals = (items || []).filter(v => String(v || '').trim());
  if (!vals.length) return emptyLabel;
  if (vals.length === 1) return vals[0];
  if (vals.length <= 3) return vals.join('、');
  return `${vals[0]}〜${vals[vals.length - 1]}`;
}
function buildPrintRangeLine() {
  if (state.dataset === 'textbook') {
    const textbooks = shortItemsForPrint(uniqueFromCurrentList('textbook'), '全教科書');
    const grades = shortItemsForPrint(uniqueFromCurrentList('grade').map(normalizeGradeLabelForPrint), '全学年');
    const sections = shortItemsForPrint(uniqueFromCurrentList('section'), '全単元');
    return `${textbooks}｜${grades}｜${sections}`;
  }
  if (state.dataset === 'exam') {
    const cats = shortItemsForPrint(uniqueFromCurrentList('exam_category'), '全範囲');
    return `高校入試版｜${cats}`;
  }
  const types = shortItemsForPrint(uniqueFromCurrentList('elem_type'), '全範囲');
  const cats = shortItemsForPrint(uniqueFromCurrentList('elem_category'), '全カテゴリー');
  return `小学生版｜${types}｜${cats}`;
}
